## Apply semantics

| Class | Incident casualty result | Verification | Reviewable destination | Audit reason_code |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| corrected | replace with validated local claims | unchanged unless an existing conflict applies | source evidence and incident update | repair |
| unchanged | no change | no change | existing incident/source evidence | none |
| needs_reextraction | no change | no automatic flag change | report/re-extraction queue candidate | none |
| source_missing | no change | no automatic flag change | report for source recovery | none |
| village_mismatch | remove values from the wrong village | needs_verification with owning village(s) | connected source claims and incident update | repair |

| skipped_admin_edited | no change | preserve human decision | existing human edit audit | none |
| failed | rolled back | no change | failure row | none |
